===== research/wp1d/WP1D-completion-report.md SHA256 38CAE8C01AE7D05EF93E99F0FF6DFBD701357376A9E13695A92E9132D2C7A30B =====
# WP1D Completion Report
Revision0.1 •2026-09-21 • COMPLETE FOR REVIEW, pending Gate1D. No next package authorized.

## Delivered
1. [Reserve Architecture Research Report](01-reserve-architecture-report.md)
2. [Reserve Eligibility Specification](02-reserve-eligibility.md)
3. [Candidate Reserve Structures](03-candidate-structures.md)
4. [Duration and Liquidity Analysis](04-duration-liquidity.md)
5. [Inflation-Matching and Basis-Risk Analysis](05-inflation-basis-risk.md)
6. [Haircut Framework](06-haircut-framework.md)
7. [Custody / Segregation / Encumbrance Requirements](07-custody-segregation.md)
8. [Adversarial Failure Analysis](08-adversarial-failure-analysis.md)
9. [Alternative Asset Comparison](09-alternative-assets.md)
10. [Parameter and Assumption Register](10-parameters.md)
11. [Sources](11-sources.md), [model](reserve_model.py), [tests](test_reserve_model.py), [scenario generator](calculate_scenarios.py), [results](scenario-results.json), [validation](validation.json)
12. [Claude input/findings/reconciliation](../../reviews/gate1d/claude-reconciliation.md), [Gate submission](../../reviews/gate1d/Gate-1D-submission.md), [manifest](../../reviews/gate1d/submission-manifest.json), updated canonical records/change log.

## Outcome and limits
Conditional cash/bill/short-TIPS and controlled cash-lender Treasury repo deserve further falsification. No architecture here is shown to satisfy perpetual CPI-linked liabilities under every plausible roll, rate, liquidity, custody and benchmark state. Negative net real carry exhausts finite resources; runs can crystallize losses; title/access and Q023 remain unresolved. Numerical allocation, duration, liquidity, haircut and alternative-return parameters remain hypotheses, not selected production values. No new fallback or true-up; no capital instrument, compensation, optimal ratio, AP, mint/redemption, entity, bankruptcy priority, custodian, chain or contract design.

Original roadmap/draft source and accepted WP1B/WP1C calculation code preserved. Current canonical handoff records SRC019 approval of WP1C0.1 WITH CONDITIONS based on direct user evidence, not independent primary-transcript retrieval. Prior manifests remain historical byte scopes. All explicitly stated conditions and Q021–Q025 remain OPEN where required; Q023 blocks production activation.

## Validation and reviewer scope
Run from repository root with Python3: `python research/wp1d/test_reserve_model.py` and `python research/wp1d/calculate_scenarios.py`; run the three WP1B suites and WP1C suite unchanged, then `python tools/validate_repository.py` against the current manifest. Exact-fraction assertions test model identities and negative/boundary inputs; generated scenarios illustrate sensitivity rather than validate their empirical assumptions. Counts and results are in the Gate submission and machine-readable reports.

No production certification, economic viability proof, legal validation, solvency proof, oracle-security proof, adoption evidence or independent professional audit. Claude is an independent AI adversarial reviewer of supplied inputs, not a human reviewer or Gate authority. See exact bundle scope/revisions and unresolved objections in the reconciliation. No new external human/professional review occurred for WP1D.

Stop at Gate1D. Brad's explicit approval is required before WP1E or any later package.

## Current validation evidence
124 WP1D exact-model diagnostic assertions and20 artifact-reproduction/scenario assertions pass. These are separate from the unchanged220 WP1B and58 WP1C checks. Assertion count is not a count of independent experiments or certification. Model invariants, direct expected-value counterexamples, input failures, paired-claim identities, horizon boundaries and deterministic regeneration have distinct scopes.


===== reviews/gate1d/claude-reconciliation.md SHA256 E576F2308CE5786AAEAE54BCF4BC7EE1FF87AC2526EC8640EEA8093231587D7D =====
# Claude adversarial review and operator reconciliation
Date2026-09-21. UI model label **Opus 5 High**; underlying model build unknown. Review conducted in the signed-in Claude browser, no human reviewer or Gate approval. Exact prompts are preserved; attached bundles bind scope. Findings below are attributed operator paraphrases of Claude's rendered response, not a formal platform export. No private conversation/account URLs are published.

## Stage1 — initial draft
Input: [exact prompt](stage1-input.txt). Fifteen material findings received and independently reconciled below. Evidence refers to the WP1B/WP1C accepted records and WP1D source/analysis files.

| ID | Claude finding | Disposition and independent basis | Remaining issue |
|---|---|---|---|
| C1-01 | Negative real carry makes instrument set structurally insolvent | PARTLY ACCEPTED: finite assets erode under persistently negative net real carry; arithmetic120*.98^10<100. Universal insolvency claim rejected; positive real returns/adequate funding can change result. Specific historical yield ranges not independently verified or used | Sustainable funding WP1E/Phase2 OPEN |
| C1-02 | PPU uses newer CPI than TIPS, producing systematic lag gap | REJECTED premise: approved WP1B adapted daily lagged calculation is not assumed latest-release step. ACCEPTED possibility of dated vintage/contingency basis; document05 and Treasury2025 evidence | Exact empirical matching Phase2; no target rewrite |
| C1-03 |18-place precision creates noise/rounding exploitation | PARTLY ACCEPTED: arithmetic precision is not measurement accuracy. WP1B defines binding arithmetic; no per-payment round-half-up assumed. Reject implicit alteration of accepted mechanics and issuer-favorable rounding selection | Payment granularity/timing WP1F/later technical work |
| C1-04 | Fixed accepted claims break deflation matching | ACCEPTED branch risk, not universal failure: fixed faces92 versus indexed90 example. Nominal holdings can offset, but no queue or legal segregation choice adopted | WP1F/Phase2 |
| C1-05 | Scalar Qidx collapses vintages | REJECTED for accepted per-version PPU-unit definition:100 units times target is intentional remeasurement, not original-dollar accrual. Different ratio/fee contracts require separate typed obligations, already WP1C | Q025/perimeter proof OPEN |
| C1-06 | Impairment leaves undefined T; everybody/nobody redeems by direction | PARTLY ACCEPTED: stale target can create timing incentives. Reject necessary undefined T (binding carried target exists) and deterministic behavior forecasts. Opposite directions documented, no option price or remedy invented | Q022/Q023/Q024 OPEN |
| C1-07 | Sequential service defeats positive starting buffer | ACCEPTED: run curve and cash20/security100/demand80/20%cost example destroys15 buffer. No asset-only run-proof claim | WP1F behavioral remedies/Phase2 |
| C1-08 | Bank cash is credit exposure not assured immediate par | ACCEPTED: FDIC limits and blocked-bank horizon branch; reject unconditional ban on every usable uninsured dollar as a sourced legal rule | Actual insurance/access/legal evidence required |
| C1-09 | Real-yield and liquidity haircuts can double count2008 | ACCEPTED control: total-return factor plus only incremental execution cost. Historical prices not combined with independently duplicated premia | Joint calibration Phase2 |
| C1-10 | Term reverse repo unavailable before maturity | ACCEPTED absent legally available unwind/closeout; no renewal/predefault collateral-sale assumption | Closeout access and time Phase3 |
| C1-11 | Seasoned TIPS floor is inoperative | PARTLY ACCEPTED: not a purchase-price/early-sale guarantee. Universal 'inoperative' rejected; contractual maturity floor still exists, moneyness instrument-specific | No floor benefit priced in proxy |
| C1-12 | Matrix marks are not verified executable prices | ACCEPTED: hierarchy, as-of/freshness and exception controls in07 | Actual limits/feeds later |
| C1-13 | Excluding encumbrance without associated debt understates claims | ACCEPTED paired full-inventory reconciliation; subtracting entire debt again after collateral exclusion can also double count. Separate pool allocation with residual deficiency explicit | Legal allocation unknown |
| C1-14 | All instruments share sovereign dependence | ACCEPTED common sovereign/benchmark dependence; cash also has bank dependency. Weight changes do more than duration alone but do not diversify sovereign | Institutional risk remains OPEN |
| C1-15 | Direct bills/TIPS dominate absent compensating utility | PARTLY ACCEPTED: compare costs and utility; domination not universal theorem without user preferences. No demonstrated programming/transfer utility demand claimed | Q021 OPEN |

No disputed reviewer claim is promoted into an established fact merely because Claude asserted it. Later-package deferral identifies a needed answer, not authorization to obtain it now.

## Stage2 — full draft and quantitative model
Input: [exact prompt](stage2-input.txt) plus [exact attached draft bundle](stage2-review-bundle.txt). The bundle intentionally remains unchanged as review evidence, including its subsequently corrected defects. Claude reported93/93 passing on its rerun; operator verification is separately recorded. Eighteen material findings received. Revised core remains revision0.1 before its first Gate submission; change history and exact review bytes distinguish iterations.

===== reviews/gate1d/Gate-1D-submission.md SHA256 AABAF3C8B8F76CAB78901E4B28C07658CAEF009AC2E9C762B491B89A2B473698 =====
# Gate1D — Reserve Architecture submission
Revision0.1 •2026-09-21. **AWAITING REVIEW / NOT APPROVED.** WP1D COMPLETE FOR REVIEW. Next authorized Work Package: **NONE**. **WP1E and all later packages NOT AUTHORIZED.** Phase Gate1 not reached.

## Decision requested from primary review
Accept, revise or reject D018's candidate eligibility/measurement framework and dependency mapping for continued research. This is not a request to approve an allocation, safe collateral ratio, production reserve, capital instrument or transaction/legal design. Gate0/1A/1B conditions persist. Gate1C0.1 is recorded APPROVED WITH CONDITIONS on SRC019 user evidence; no missing primary-review wording is invented.

| Required Gate question | Research answer |
|---|---|
| What assets are eligible? | Conditional baseline candidates: usable USD cash, direct short bills, controlled short Treasury reverse repo as cash lender, direct short remaining-duration TIPS. Evidence/freshness/title/access predicates must hold; no production asset approval |
| What assets are excluded and why? | Leverage/rehypothecation/unverified or encumbered free credit excluded; alternatives and wrappers are research-only because extra basis/tail/access/legal/operational risks are unproven improvements |
| How much liquidity is conceptually required? | Enough actually accessible USD at each deadline to cover stressed obligations, operating cash and explicit uncertainty margin, without assumed new inflows/renewal/borrowing. No universal percentage before WP1F terms |
| What duration is tolerable? | Must fit a joint loss budget and horizon test.0–2-year TIPS candidate bracket contrasted with0.5/2/5/10-year diagnostics; no calibrated safe ceiling |
| How closely can reserves match liability? | Ordinary index path can align partially; nominal sleeve, real yields, cash flows, costs, vintage/contingency and fixed-claim mix prevent exact general matching |
| Where does unavoidable basis remain? | Government methodology/contingency, published-versus-current prices, fixed/indexed accepted claims, maturity/roll, market liquidity and deadline access |
| What if real yields rise sharply? | TIPS can lose sale value despite inflation accrual; long-duration proxy loses much more. Full instrument repricing/calibration required later |
| What in a redemption run? | Sell sufficient marked assets to cover net proceeds; losses reduce remaining buffer and may leave unpaid claims. No asset-only run guarantee or invented fee/gate remedy |
| What if benchmark impaired? | Show reference liability with impairment disclosure; no remedy, compensation, fallback or distributable-surplus inference. Q023 blocks production activation |
| What losses reach first-loss capital? | Economic asset losses, expenses and liability growth reduce residual A-L-O. Contributed capital already financed A; never add it again. Actual instruments/priority/recapitalization unchosen |
| What waits for WP1E/Phase2? | Sustainable funding and capital terms; fitted joint stresses, curve/coupon pricing, concentration/loss budgets, optimum ratios and allocations. WP1F outflow/claim terms and Phase3 title/access separately required |
| Which parameters remain hypotheses? | All weights, durations, liquidity horizons/margins, haircuts, stress magnitudes, recoveries and alternative additions; [register](../../research/wp1d/10-parameters.md) |

Q025 OPEN.** Q021/Q022/Q024, Treasury contingency-methodology conflict, institutional dependence, Q026 reserve calibration, Q027 sustainable funding and Q028 enforceable title/access remain OPEN. No fallback benchmark or holder true-up adopted; no user-demand claim.

No fresh external human/professional review of WP1D occurred.
